a7-a5 seems like a strong start. It removes one pawn from the 7th rank and keeps it away from the White King.

The rook is on the 7th rank. Any pawn that moves to the 6th (or below) is safe from the rook!

Not adjacent. So a5 is safe from the king.

1. Move the a-pawn to a5 (safest distance from King and Rook).
